we can inductively conclude that

\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\|\bm{S}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}-\bm{S}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}\|_{F}=0\quad\mbox{and}\quad\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}-\bm{\Sigma}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}\|_{F}=0.

Hence, the first assertion follows using the pseudo-Lipschitz property of
\psi
. The second assertion also follows using similar arguments.

F.3
Proof of Lemma
F.1

Let us consider the functions

\displaystyle u^{\perp,\circ}_{t,h}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)

(F.72)

\displaystyle=u^{\circ}_{t,h}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)

(F.73)

\displaystyle\hskip 15.00002pt-{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\,\mathbb{E}_{\mu}[Y_{0,h}u^{\circ}_{t,h}(\{\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}U_{h}+\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}Y_{0,h}+(\bm{T}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{1/2}W^{\perp,L}_{t,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\widetilde{Y}_{0,\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}})^{\top}],

(F.74)

and

\displaystyle v^{\perp,\circ}_{t,h}(y;\nu_{h})=v^{\circ}_{t,h}(y;\nu_{h})-{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\,\mathbb{E}_{\nu_{h}}[Y^{R}_{0,h}v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h}V_{h}+\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h}Y^{R}_{0,h}+(\bm{T}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{1/2}W^{\perp,R}_{t,h})^{\top}].

(F.75)

For all
h\in[m]
, let us define the sequence of auxiliary iterations
\{\widebar{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h},\widebar{\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}\}_{t\geq 0}
as follows:

\displaystyle\widebar{\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}=v^{\perp,\circ}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}+\bm{V}_{h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top};\nu_{h}),

(F.76)

\displaystyle{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}=\bm{W}^{\perp}_{h}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}-\widebar{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t-1,h}\,\gamma_{h}\left(\bm{J}^{R,\perp}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}+\bm{V}_{h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top};\nu_{h})\right)^{\top},

(F.77)

\displaystyle\widebar{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}=u^{\perp,\circ}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,1}+\bm{U}_{1}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,1})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,1}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,1})^{\top},\ldots,

(F.78)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,m}+\bm{U}_{m}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,m})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,m}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,m})^{\top},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu),

(F.79)

\displaystyle{\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t+1,h}=(\bm{W}^{\perp}_{h})^{\top}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}-\widebar{\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}\,\bigg(\bm{J}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,1}+\bm{U}_{1}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,1})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,1}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,1})^{\top},\ldots,

(F.80)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,m}+\bm{U}_{m}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,m})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,m}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,m})^{\top},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)\bigg)^{\top}.

(F.81)

In the above definitions
\{\bm{W}^{\perp}_{h}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times p_{h}}:(\bm{W}^{\perp}_{h})_{ij}\overset{iid}{\sim}N(0,N^{-1}),h\in[m]\}
and is independent of the observed data. Further, the matrix-valued functions
\bm{J}^{R,\perp}_{t,h}(\cdot):\mathbb{R}^{p_{h}\times r_{h}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}\times r_{h}}
is defined similarly as (
5.14
) with
v^{\perp,\circ}_{t,h}(\cdot)
replacing
v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\cdot)
. Similarly, the matrix-valued functions
\bm{J}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}(\cdot):\prod_{k=1}^{m}\mathbb{R}^{N\times r_{k}}\times\prod_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\mathbb{R}^{N\times{\widetilde{r}}_{\ell}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}\times r_{h}}
is defined similarly as (
5.15
) with
u^{\perp,\circ}_{t,h}(\cdot)
replacing
u^{\circ}_{t,h}(\cdot)
.
We initialize the iterations (
F.76
) as follows:

\displaystyle{\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{0,h}=\bm{V}^{\perp}_{0,h}-\bm{V}_{h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{0,h})^{\top}-{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{0,h})^{\top}=0,

(F.82)

\displaystyle{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{-1,h}=\bm{U}^{\perp}_{-1,h}-\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{-1,h})^{\top}-{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{-1,h})^{\top}=0,

(F.83)

for all
h\in[m]
. Now we consider the following lemma.

Lemma F.3
.

Let
u^{\mathtt{g}}_{t,h}:\mathbb{R}^{r+\widetilde{r}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}}
,
v^{\mathtt{g}}_{t,h}:\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}}
be Lipschitz functions with Lipschitz Jacobian matrices. Let
\bm{G}_{h}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times p_{h}}
for
h\in[m]
be random matrices with iid Gaussian entries with zero mean and variance
N^{-1}
. The matrices
\bm{G}_{h}
are independent of one another for
h\in[m]
. Consider the sequence of AMP iterates defined as follows:

\displaystyle\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}=v^{\mathtt{g}}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}),

(F.84)

\displaystyle{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h}=\bm{G}_{h}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}-\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t-1,h}\,\gamma_{h}\left(J^{\mathtt{g,R}}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h})\right)^{\top},

(F.85)

\displaystyle\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h}=u^{\mathtt{g}}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,1},\ldots,{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}}),

(F.86)

\displaystyle{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t+1,h}=\bm{G}^{\top}_{h}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h}-\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}\,\left(J^{\mathtt{g,L}}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,1},\ldots,{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})\right)^{\top}.

(F.87)

where
{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h}=\bm{0}
and
\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{-1,h}=\bm{0}
. In the above definitions, the matrix-valued functions
J^{\mathtt{g,R}}_{t,h}:\mathbb{R}^{p_{h}\times r_{h}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}\times r_{h}}
corresponding to the function
v^{\mathtt{g}}_{t,h}(\bm{x})
is defined as follows:

J^{\mathtt{g,R}}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h})=\frac{1}{p_{h}}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{h}}\frac{\bm{\partial}v^{\mathtt{g}}_{t,h}}{\bm{\partial}\bm{x}}(({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h})_{i*})

Similarly, the matrix-valued functions
J^{\mathtt{g,L}}_{t,h}:\prod_{k=1}^{m}\mathbb{R}^{N\times r_{k}}\times\prod_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\mathbb{R}^{N\times{\widetilde{r}}_{\ell}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}\times r_{h}}
corresponding to the function
u^{\mathtt{g}}_{t,h}(\bm{x}_{1},\ldots,\bm{x}_{m},\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{x}}_{\widetilde{m}})
is defined as follows:

\displaystyle J^{\mathtt{g,L}}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,1},\ldots,{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})

(F.88)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\bm{\partial}u^{\mathtt{g}}_{t,h}}{\bm{\partial}\bm{x}_{h}}(({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*}).

(F.89)

Next, consider any sequence of deterministic subsets
\mathcal{F}_{L}\subset[N]
satisfying
\frac{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}{N}\rightarrow\lambda_{L}\in(0,1]
, as
N\rightarrow\infty
and
\{\mathcal{F}_{R,h}\subset[p_{h}]:h\in[m]\}
satisfying
\frac{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}{p_{h}}\rightarrow\lambda_{R,h}\in(0,1]
, as
p_{h}\rightarrow\infty
for all
h\in[m]
.
Then, for any pseudo Lipschitz functions
\psi:\mathbb{R}^{3r+2\widetilde{r}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
and
\phi_{h}:\mathbb{R}^{3r_{h}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
we have:

\displaystyle\hskip-2.5pt\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\left(\{({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\right)

(F.90)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt-\mathbb{E}_{\mu}\left[\psi\left(\{(\bm{T}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{1/2}W^{\mathtt{g,L}}_{t,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\widetilde{Y}_{0,\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{Y_{0,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{U_{h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\widetilde{U}_{\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\right)\right]\Bigg|=0,

(F.91)

and

\displaystyle\lim_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{R,h}}\phi_{h}\left(({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{0,h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}\right)

(F.92)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi\left((\bm{T}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{1/2}W^{\mathtt{g,R}}_{t,h},V_{0,h},V_{h}\right)\right]\Bigg|=0.

(F.93)

Here,
W^{\mathtt{g,L}}_{t,1},\ldots,W^{\mathtt{g,L}}_{t,m}
and
W^{\mathtt{g,R}}_{t,1},\ldots,W^{\mathtt{g,R}}_{t,\widetilde{m}}
are independent;
W^{\mathtt{g,L}}_{t,h},W^{\mathtt{g,R}}_{t,h}\sim N_{r_{h}}(0,\bm{I}_{r_{h}})
for
h\in[m]
,
V_{h}\sim\nu_{h}
,
(U_{1},\ldots,U_{m},\widetilde{U}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{U}_{\widetilde{m}})\sim\mu
and for all
h\in[m]
, the parameters
\bm{T}^{G,L}_{t,h}
and
\bm{T}^{G,R}_{t,h}
are defined as follows:

\displaystyle\bm{T}^{G,L}_{t,h}=\gamma_{h}\mathbb{E}[v^{\mathtt{g}}_{t,h}((\bm{T}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{1/2}W^{\mathtt{g,R}}_{t,h})^{\otimes 2}],\quad\mbox{and}

(F.94)

\displaystyle\bm{T}^{G,R}_{t+1,h}=\mathbb{E}[u^{\mathtt{g}}_{t,h}((\bm{T}^{G,L}_{t,1})^{1/2}W^{\mathtt{g,L}}_{t,1},\ldots,(\bm{T}^{G,L}_{t,m})^{1/2}W^{\mathtt{g,L}}_{t,m},\widetilde{Y}_{0,1},\ldots,\widetilde{Y}_{0,\widetilde{m}})^{\otimes 2}],

(F.95)

where
\bm{T}^{G,R}_{0,h}=\bm{0}
.

Observe that the AMP orbits defined by (
F.76
) conform to the set-up described in Lemma
F.3
. Hence, we can conclude that for any sequence of pseudo-Lipschitz functions
\widetilde{\psi}:\mathbb{R}^{3r+2\widetilde{r}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
and
\widetilde{\phi}_{h}:\mathbb{R}^{3r_{h}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
, almost surely we have:

\displaystyle\hskip-5.0pt\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\tilde{\psi}\left(\{({\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\right)

(F.96)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt-\mathbb{E}_{\mu}\left[\widetilde{\psi}\left(\{(\bm{T}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{1/2}W^{\perp,L}_{t,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{Y_{0,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\widetilde{Y}_{0,\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{U_{h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\widetilde{U}_{\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\right)\right]\Bigg|=0,

(F.97)

and

\displaystyle\lim_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{R,h}}\widetilde{\phi}_{h}\left(({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h})_{i*},({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}\right)

(F.98)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-\mathbb{E}_{\nu_{h}}\left[\widetilde{\phi}_{h}\left((\bm{T}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{1/2}W^{\perp,R}_{t,h},Y^{R}_{0,h},V_{h}\right)\right]\Bigg|=0,

(F.99)

where
\{\bm{T}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}:h\in[m]\}
and
\{\bm{T}^{R,\perp}_{t,h}:h\in[m]\}
are defined by (
F.32
). Let us choose the following sequence of pseudo-Lipschitz functions:

\displaystyle\tilde{\psi}\left(\{({\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\right)

(F.100)

\displaystyle\quad=\psi\bigg(\{({\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h})_{i*}+\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}+\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},

(F.101)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\bigg)

(F.102)

\displaystyle\tilde{\phi}_{h}\left(({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h})_{i*},({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}\right)

(F.103)

\displaystyle\quad=\phi_{h}\left(({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h})_{i*}+\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h}(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}+\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h}({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*},({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}\right)

(F.104)

Consequently, for all
h\in[m]
, almost surely we have

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\bigg(\{({\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}+\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},

(F.105)

\displaystyle\hskip 120.00018pt\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\bigg)

(F.106)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt-\mathbb{E}\left[\psi\left(\{\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}U_{h}+\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}Y_{0,h}+(\bm{T}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{1/2}W^{\perp,L}_{t,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{Y_{0,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\right.\right.

(F.107)

\displaystyle\hskip 140.00021pt\left.\left.\{\widetilde{Y}_{0,\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{U_{h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\widetilde{U}_{\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\right)\right]\Bigg|=0,

(F.108)

and

\displaystyle\lim_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{R,h}}\phi_{h}\left(({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h})_{i*},({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}\right)

(F.109)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt-\mathbb{E}_{\nu_{h}}\left[\phi_{h}\left(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h}V_{h}+\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h}Y^{R}_{0,h}+(\bm{T}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{1/2}W^{\perp,R}_{t,h},Y^{R}_{0,h},V_{h}\right)\right]\Bigg|=0.

(F.110)

So, to establish the result, it is enough to show:

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\bigg(\{({\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}+\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},

\displaystyle\hskip 140.00021pt\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\bigg)

(F.111)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt-\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\bigg(\{(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{d=1}^{m},

(F.112)

\displaystyle\hskip 140.00021pt\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\bigg)\Bigg|=0,

(F.113)

and

\displaystyle\lim_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{R,h}}\phi_{h}\left(({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}+\bm{V}_{h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top})_{i*},({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt-\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{R,h}}\phi_{h}\left((\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})_{i*},({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0.h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}\right)\Bigg|=0.

(F.114)

To show (
F.111
) and (
F.114
), for
h\in[m]
, we define the discrepancy between the sets of iterates
\{\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,h},\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h}\}_{t\geq 0}
and
\{{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}+\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top},{\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}+\bm{V}_{h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}\}_{t\geq 0}
as follows.

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}_{1,t,h}

\displaystyle=\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,h}-({\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}+\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}),

(F.115)

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}_{2,t,h}

\displaystyle=\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h}-({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}+\bm{V}_{h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}).

(F.116)

We shall inductively show the following:

1.

For any sequence of deterministic subsets
\mathcal{F}_{L}\subset[N]
satisfying
\frac{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}{N}\rightarrow\lambda_{L}\in(0,1]
, as
N\rightarrow\infty
and pseudo-Lipschitz function
\psi:\mathbb{R}^{3r+2\widetilde{r}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
, for all
t\geq-1
, almost surely

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\bigg(\{({\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}+\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},

(F.117)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\bigg)

(F.118)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\left(\{(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\right.

(F.119)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt\left.\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\right)\Bigg|=0,

(F.120)

2.

For any sequence of deterministic subsets
\mathcal{F}_{R,h}\subset[p_{h}]
satisfying
\frac{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}{p_{h}}\rightarrow\lambda_{R,h}\in(0,1]
, as
p_{h}\rightarrow\infty
and any pseudo-Lipschitz functions
\phi_{h}:\mathbb{R}^{3r_{h}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
for
h\in[m]
, for all
t\geq-1
, almost surely

\displaystyle\lim_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{R,h}}\phi_{h}\left(({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}+\bm{V}_{h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top})_{i*},({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}\right)

(F.121)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt-\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{R,h}}\phi_{h}\left((\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{0.h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}\right)\Bigg|=0.

(F.122)

3.

For
t\geq-1
, we have

\lim\limits_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{N}\|\bm{\Delta}_{1,t,h}\|^{2}_{F}=0.

4.

For
t\geq-1
and for all
h\in[m]
, we have

\lim\limits_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{p_{h}}\|\bm{\Delta}_{2,t,h}\|^{2}_{F}=0.

5.

For
t\geq-1
, we have

\lim\limits_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{N}\|\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}<\infty,\quad\mbox{and}\quad\lim\limits_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{N}\|{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}+\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}<\infty.

6.

For
t\geq 0
and
h\in[m]
, we have

\lim\limits_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{p_{h}}\|\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t+1,h}\|^{2}_{F}<\infty,\;\mbox{and}\;\lim\limits_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{p_{h}}\|{\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t+1,h}+\bm{V}_{h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t+1,h})^{\top}+{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t+1,h})^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}<\infty.

To show the above assertions, we first introduce the following notations:

\displaystyle f_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})=u^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})-\bm{P}_{{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}\widebar{\bm{U}}^{\perp}_{t,h},

(F.123)

\displaystyle g_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})=v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})-\bm{P}_{{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}\widebar{\bm{V}}^{\perp}_{t,h},

(F.124)

\displaystyle\bm{\delta}_{t,1,h}=\bm{P}_{{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}(\bm{W}^{\perp}_{h})^{\top}f_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}}),\quad\bm{\delta}_{t,2,h}=\bm{P}_{{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}\bm{W}^{\perp}_{h}g_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h}).

(F.125)

Here,
\bm{P}_{{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}=N^{-1}{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}
and
\bm{P}_{{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}=p_{h}^{-1}{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}
.
Observe that for
t\geq 0
and
h\in[m]
:

\displaystyle\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,h}

\displaystyle={\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\bigg[\bm{D}_{0,h}\frac{({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})}{N}-\frac{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}\widebar{\bm{U}}^{\perp}_{t-1,h}}{N}\gamma_{h}\left(\bm{J}^{R,\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h};\nu_{h})\right)^{\top}

(F.126)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-\frac{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}\bm{U}_{h}}{N}\bigg(\frac{\bm{D}_{h}\bm{V}^{\top}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})}{N}\bigg)\bigg]

(F.127)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\bm{U}_{h}\bm{D}_{h}\bm{V}^{\top}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})

(F.128)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt-f_{t-1,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t-1,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t-1,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})\gamma_{h}\left(\bm{J}^{R,\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h};\nu_{h})\right)^{\top}

(F.129)

\displaystyle\quad+\bm{W}^{\perp}_{h}g_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})-\bm{\delta}_{t,2,h},

(F.130)

\displaystyle\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h}

\displaystyle={\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\bigg[\bm{D}_{0,h}\frac{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}u^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})}{N}

(F.131)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt-\frac{({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}\widebar{\bm{V}}^{\perp}_{t,h}}{p_{h}}\left(\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})\right)^{\top}

(F.132)

\displaystyle\hskip 60.00009pt-\frac{({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}\bm{V}_{h}}{p_{h}}\bigg(\frac{\bm{D}_{h}\bm{U}^{\top}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}u^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})}{N}\bigg)\bigg]

(F.133)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\bm{V}_{h}\bm{D}_{h}\bm{U}^{\top}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}u^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})

(F.134)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt+(\bm{W}^{\perp}_{h})^{\top}f_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})

(F.135)

\displaystyle\quad-g_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})\left(\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})\right)^{\top}-\bm{\delta}_{t,1,h},

(F.136)

with the convention
u^{\perp,\circ}_{-1,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{-1,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{-1,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})=\widebar{\bm{U}}^{\perp}_{-1,h}
. Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}_{t,1,h}

\displaystyle={\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\bigg[\bm{D}_{0,h}\frac{({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})}{N}-\frac{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}\widebar{\bm{U}}^{\perp}_{t-1,h}}{N}\gamma_{h}\left(\bm{J}^{R,\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h};\nu_{h})\right)^{\top}

(F.137)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}\bm{U}_{h}}{N}\bigg(\frac{\bm{D}_{h}\bm{V}^{\top}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})}{N}\bigg)-(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}\bigg]

(F.138)

\displaystyle\qquad+\bm{U}_{h}\bigg[\bm{D}_{h}\frac{\bm{V}^{\top}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})}{N}-(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}\bigg]

(F.139)

\displaystyle\qquad+\bm{W}^{\perp}_{h}(g_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})-v^{\perp,\circ}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}+\bm{V}_{h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}))

(F.140)

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\widebar{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t-1,h}\,\gamma_{h}\left(\bm{J}^{R,\perp}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}+\bm{V}_{h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top};\nu_{h})\right)^{\top}

(F.141)

\displaystyle\qquad-f_{t-1,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t-1,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t-1,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})\gamma_{h}\left(\bm{J}^{R,\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h};\nu_{h})\right)^{\top}\bigg)-\bm{\delta}_{t,2,h}

(F.142)

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}_{t+1,2,h}

\displaystyle={\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\bigg[\bm{D}_{0,h}\frac{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}u^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})}{N}

(F.143)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}\widebar{\bm{V}}^{\perp}_{t,h}}{p_{h}}(\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}},\mu))^{\top}

(F.144)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}\bm{V}_{h}}{p_{h}}\bigg(\frac{\bm{D}_{h}\bm{U}^{\top}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}u^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})}{N}\bigg)-(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}\bigg]

(F.145)

\displaystyle+\bm{V}_{h}\bigg[\bm{D}_{h}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}u^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})}{N}-(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}\bigg]-\bm{\delta}_{t,2,h}

(F.146)

\displaystyle+(\bm{W}^{\perp}_{h})^{\top}\bigg(f_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})

(F.147)

\displaystyle-u^{\perp,\circ}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,1}+\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,1})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,1}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,1})^{\top},\ldots,

(F.148)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,m}+\bm{U}_{m}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,m})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,m}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,m})^{\top},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})\bigg)

(F.149)

\displaystyle+\bigg(\widebar{\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}\,\bigg(\bm{J}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,1}+\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top},\ldots,

(F.150)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}+\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})\bigg)^{\top}

(F.151)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-g_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})\left(\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})\right)^{\top}\bigg)

(F.152)

The base case for
t=-1
follows by the definitions of
\bm{U}^{\perp}_{-1,h}
and
\bm{V}^{\perp}_{0,h}
. Let us assume that the assertions (1)-(6) hold for
t=-1,\ldots,r
. First, let us consider Assertion (3) for
t=r+1
. Using (
F.32
), the assertions (2), (4), (6) for
t=r
and arguments similar to the proof of (B.46) of
Montanari and Venkataramanan, [39]
, we can show

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\bigg[\bm{D}_{0,h}\frac{({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})}{N}-\frac{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}\widebar{\bm{U}}^{\perp}_{t-1,h}}{N}\gamma_{h}\left(\bm{J}^{R,\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h};\nu_{h})\right)^{\top}

(F.154)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt-\frac{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{\top}\bm{U}_{h}}{N}\bigg(\frac{\bm{D}_{h}\bm{V}^{\top}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})}{N}\bigg)-(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}\bigg]=0

(F.155)

Using the arguments similar to the proof of (B.47) of
Montanari and Venkataramanan, [39]
and (
F.32
), we can further show that for all
h\in[m]

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\bigg[\bm{D}_{h}\frac{\bm{V}^{\top}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}}v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})}{N}-(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}\bigg]=0.

(F.156)

Using the fact that
\|\bm{W}^{\perp}_{h}\|^{2}\rightarrow 4
along with the Lipschitz property of the functions
v^{\circ}_{0,h}
’s and their derivatives and the assertions (2), (4), (5), (6) for
t=r-1
, we can show along the lines of the proof of (B.48) and (B.49) of
Montanari and Venkataramanan, [39]
that

\lim\limits_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{N}\|\bm{W}^{\perp}_{h}(g_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h})-v^{\perp,\circ}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}+\bm{V}_{h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}))\|_{F}=0,

and

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{N}\bigg\|\widebar{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t-1,h}\,\gamma_{h}\left(\bm{J}^{R,\perp}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{\perp,R}_{t,h}+\bm{V}_{h}(\bm{A}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{R,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top};\nu_{h})\right)^{\top}

(F.157)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt-f_{t-1,h}(\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t-1,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t-1,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})\gamma_{h}\left(\bm{J}^{R,\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{V}^{\perp}_{t,h};\nu_{h})\right)^{\top}\bigg\|_{F}=0.

(F.158)

Again, using the Lipschitz property of the denoisers and the previously established assertions, we can prove along the lines of (B.50) of
Montanari and Venkataramanan, [39]
that
\lim\limits_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{N}\|\bm{\delta}_{t,2,h}\|=0
.
This proves assertion (3).

Next, we consider Assertion (5) for
t=r+1
. Consider the pseudo Lipschitz function
\psi(x,u,u_{0})=\|x+\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}u+\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h}u_{0}\|^{2}
. Then by Lemma
F.3
, we have

\lim\limits_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{N}\|{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}+\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}<\infty.

Then, using Assertion (3) for
t=r+1
, we get

\lim\limits_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{N}\|\bm{U}^{\perp}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}<\infty.

To prove assertion (1), observe that, since
\frac{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}{N}\rightarrow\lambda_{L}\in(0,1]
, using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and properties of pseudo Lipschitz functions (
5.6
), we can get a constant
C>0
(possibly depending on
\lambda_{L}
),

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\bigg(\{({\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}+\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},

\displaystyle\hskip 150.00023pt\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\bigg)

(F.159)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt-\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\left(\{(\widebar{\bm{U}}^{\perp}_{t,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\right.

(F.160)

\displaystyle\hskip 150.00023pt\left.\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\right)\Bigg|

(F.161)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg(1+\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\|\bm{\Delta}_{t,h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}+\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\|\bm{U}_{h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}+\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\|{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}

(F.162)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt+\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\|{\bm{N}}^{\perp,L}_{t,h}+\bm{U}_{h}(\bm{A}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}+{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}(\bm{B}^{L,\perp}_{t,h})^{\top}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}\bigg)\times\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\|\bm{\Delta}_{t,h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}.

(F.163)

Combining Assertions (3) and (5) for
t=r+1
with Proposition
5.1
, Assertion (1) follows.
The proof of Assertion (4) follows by retracing the steps of the proof of Assertion (3) while that of Assertion (6) follows using Assertion (3) and the techniques of the proof of Assertion (5). Finally, we can show Assertion (2) using Assertions (4) and (6) and the steps of the proof of Assertion (1).

F.4
Proof of Lemma
F.3

To prove this lemma, we shall use the Gaussian conditioning technique from
Bolthausen, [9]
as in
Berthier et al., [7]
. Let us denote the
\sigma
-algebra generated by

\displaystyle\{\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h},\ldots,\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t-1,h},{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h},\ldots{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t-1,h},

(F.164)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{1,h},\ldots,\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h},{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{1,h},\ldots,{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell}:h\in[m],\,\ell\in[\widetilde{m}]\}

(F.165)

by
\mathcal{G}_{t,t}
and the
\sigma
-algebra generated by

\displaystyle\{\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{1,h},\ldots,\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h},{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{1,h},\ldots,{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h},

(F.166)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h},\ldots,\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h},{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h},\ldots,{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell}:h\in[m],\,\ell\in[\widetilde{m}]\}

(F.167)

by
\mathcal{G}_{t+1,t}
.
Let us simplify the notations by introducing the following notations:

\bm{J}^{G,R}_{t,h}=\gamma_{h}J^{\mathtt{g,R}}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}),\quad\bm{J}^{G,L}_{t,h}=J^{\mathtt{g,L}}_{t,h}({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,1},\ldots,{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}}).

Observe that conditional distribution of
\bm{G}_{h}
given
\mathcal{G}_{t+1,t}
is equivalent to the conditional distribution of
\bm{G}_{h}
given

\underbracket{[{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{1,h}+\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h}(\bm{J}^{G,L}_{0,h})^{\top}|\cdots|{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}+\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t-1,h}(\bm{J}^{G,L}_{t-1,h})^{\top}]}_{=\boldsymbol{\mathsf{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}}=\bm{G}^{\top}_{h}\underbracket{[\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h}|\cdots|\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t-1,h}]}_{=\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h}}

and

\underbracket{[{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h}|\cdots|{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h}+\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t-1,h}(\bm{J}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{\top}]}_{=\boldsymbol{\mathsf{N}}^{G,L}_{t+1,h}}=\bm{G}_{h}\underbracket{[\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h}|\cdots|\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}]}_{=\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t+1,h}}.

Using Lemma 11 and 12 of
[
5
]
, we can show that

\bm{G}_{h}|_{\mathcal{G}_{t+1,t}}\overset{d}{=}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{E}}_{t+1,t,h}+\boldsymbol{\mathsf{P}}_{t+1,t,h}(\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}),

where

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\mathsf{E}}_{t+1,t,h}=\boldsymbol{\mathsf{N}}^{G,L}_{t+1,h}((\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t+1,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t+1,h})^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t+1,h})^{\top}+\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h}((\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}

(F.168)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt-\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h}((\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{N}}^{G,L}_{t+1,h}((\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t+1,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t+1,h})^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t+1,h})^{\top},

(F.169)

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\mathsf{P}}_{t+1,t,h}(\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h})=\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h}}\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t+1,h}}.

(F.170)

Here
\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\overset{d}{=}\bm{G}_{h}
and
\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}
is independent of
\bm{G}_{h}
for all
h\in[m]
. Furthermore, conditioned on
\mathcal{G}_{t,t}
the distribution of
\bm{G}_{h}
for
h\in[m]
is given by

\bm{G}_{h}|_{\mathcal{G}_{t,t}}=\boldsymbol{\mathsf{E}}_{t,t,h}+\boldsymbol{\mathsf{P}}_{t,t,h}(\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}),

where

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\mathsf{E}}_{t,t,h}=\boldsymbol{\mathsf{N}}^{G,L}_{t,d}((\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{\top}+\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h}((\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}

(F.171)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt-\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h}((\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{N}}^{G,L}_{t,d}((\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{\top},

(F.172)

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\mathsf{P}}_{t,t,d}(\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h})=\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h}}\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h}}.

(F.173)

Again, the matrices
\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\overset{d}{=}\bm{G}_{h}
and
\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}
are independent of
\bm{G}_{h}
for all
h\in[m]
.
Note that in this analysis we are only considering the non-degenerate case when
(\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h}
and
(\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h}
are of full rank. The degenerate case can be handled by adding a small random perturbation to the denoisers as shown in Section 5.4 of
Berthier et al., [7]
.

Define the following matrices:

\displaystyle\bm{\Xi}^{L}_{t,h}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{(\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h}}{N}\bigg)^{-1}\frac{(\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{\top}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h}}{N},

(F.174)

\displaystyle\mbox{and}\quad\bm{\Xi}^{R}_{t,h}=\bigg(\frac{(\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h}}{p_{h}}\bigg)^{-1}\frac{(\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{\top}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}}{p_{h}}.

(F.175)

Following the techniques used in the proof of Lemma E.1 of
Ma and Nandy, [34]
, we can inductively show the following statements for all
t\geq 0
.

1.

If
\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}_{L,t,h}=[{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h}|\cdots|{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t-1,h}]
and
\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}_{R,t,h}=[{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{1,h}|\cdots|{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}]
, then for
h\in[m]
and
N\rightarrow\infty
, we have

\displaystyle{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h}|_{\mathcal{G}_{t,t}}=\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}_{L,t,h}\bm{\Xi}^{R}_{t,h}+\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h}}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h}+\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}}_{R,t,h}\,o(1),

(F.176)

\displaystyle{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t+1,h}|_{\mathcal{G}_{t+1,t}}=\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}_{R,t,h}\bm{\Xi}^{L}_{t,h}+\widetilde{\bm{G}}^{\top}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h}}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h}+\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}}_{L,t,h}\,o(1),

(F.177)

where the matrices
\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\overset{d}{=}\bm{G}_{h}
and
\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}
are independent of
\bm{G}_{h}
for all
h\in[m]
. Furthermore, for all
h\in[m]
, the matrices
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}}_{L,t,h}
and
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}}_{R,t,h}
is formed by Gram-Schmidt orthogonalization of the columns of
\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h}
and
\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h}
, respectively.

2.

For any sequence of deterministic subsets
\mathcal{F}_{L}\subset[N]
satisfying
\frac{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}{N}\rightarrow\lambda_{L}\in(0,1]
, as
N\rightarrow\infty
and
\mathcal{F}_{R,h}\subset[p_{h}]
, for
h\in[m]
satisfying
\frac{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}{p_{h}}\rightarrow\lambda_{R,h}\in(0,1]
, as
p_{h}\rightarrow\infty
. For any pseudo Lipschitz functions
\psi:\mathbb{R}^{3r+2\widetilde{r}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
and
\phi_{h}:\mathbb{R}^{3r_{h}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
for
h\in[m]
, we have:

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\left(\{({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 150.00023pt\left.\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}\left[\psi\left(\{(\bm{T}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{1/2}W^{\mathtt{g,L}}_{t,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{Y_{0,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\widetilde{Y}_{\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{U_{h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\widetilde{U}_{\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\right)\right]\Bigg|=0,

and

\displaystyle\lim_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{R,h}}\phi_{h}\left(({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t,h})_{i*},({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}\right)

(F.179)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi\left((\bm{T}^{G,R}_{t,h})^{1/2}W^{\mathtt{g,R}}_{t,h},Y^{R}_{0,h},V_{h}\right)\right]\Bigg|=0.

3.

For all
k,\ell\in\{0,\ldots,t\}
, we have for
h\in[m]
:

\displaystyle\lim_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{k+1,h})^{\top}{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{\ell+1,h}}{p_{h}}=\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{(\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{k,h})^{\top}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{\ell,h}}{N},

(F.181)

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{k,h})^{\top}{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{\ell,h}}{N}=\gamma_{h}\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{(\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{k,h})^{\top}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{\ell,h}}{N}.

(F.182)

4.

For all
h\in[m]
, let
\varphi_{h}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},{\widetilde{x}}_{1},\ldots,{\widetilde{x}}_{\widetilde{m}}):\mathbb{R}^{r+\widetilde{r}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
and
\vartheta_{h}(x):\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
be Lipschitz functions defined on appropriate domains with Lipschitz Jacobians. Let
\mathsf{d}\varphi_{h}
and
\mathsf{d}\vartheta_{h}
be the Jacobians of
\varphi_{h}
and
\vartheta_{h}
with respect to the
x_{h}
and
y_{h}
respectively. Then we have the following:

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{k,h})^{\top}\varphi_{h}(\{{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{\ell,j}\}_{j=1}^{m},\{\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{j^{\prime}}\}_{j^{\prime}=1}^{\widetilde{m}})}{N}

(F.183)

\displaystyle\hskip 30.00005pt=\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{k,h})^{\top}{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{\ell,h}}{N}\lim\limits_{N\rightarrow\infty}\bigg(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\partial\varphi_{h}}{\partial x_{h}}(\{({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{\ell,j})_{i*}\}_{j=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{j^{\prime}})_{i*}\}_{j^{\prime}=1}^{\widetilde{m}})\bigg)^{\top},

(F.184)

\displaystyle\lim_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{k+1,h})^{\top}\vartheta_{h}({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{k+1,h})}{p_{h}}=\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{k+1,h})^{\top}{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{\ell+1,h}}{p_{h}}\lim\limits_{N\rightarrow\infty}\bigg(\frac{1}{p_{h}}\sum_{i=1}^{p_{h}}\frac{\partial\vartheta_{h}}{\partial x}({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{k,h})\bigg)^{\top}.

(F.185)

5.

For all
h\in[m]
and
i,j\in[r_{h}]\times[r_{h}]
, we have

\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\bigg[\frac{({\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h})^{\top}{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{t,h}}{N}\bigg]_{ij}<\infty\quad\mbox{and}\quad\lim_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\bigg[\frac{({\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t+1,h})^{\top}{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{t+1,h}}{p_{h}}\bigg]_{ij}<\infty.

6.

For all
0\leq s\leq t-1
and
0\leq k\leq t
, we have positive definite matrices
\boldsymbol{\mathsf{Z}}_{s,h}\in\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}\times r_{h}}
and
\boldsymbol{\mathsf{K}}_{k,h}\in\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}\times r_{h}}
such that

\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{(\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{s,h})^{\top}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,L}_{t,h}}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{s,h}}{N}\succ\boldsymbol{\mathsf{Z}}_{s,h}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\lim_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{(\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{s,h})^{\top}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{t,h}}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{s,h}}{p_{h}}\succ\boldsymbol{\mathsf{K}}_{s,h}.

We shall show Assertions (1)-(6) inductively. For
t=0
, (
F.176
) follows using the techniques used to show Assertion (a) for
\bm{b}^{0}
in
[
34
]
. Observing that
\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}_{L,0,h}=\bm{0}
and
\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{0,h}}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h}=\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h}
, from (
F.176
) for
t=0
, we can conclude that for all
h\in[m]
, for all
i\in[N]

[{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h}]_{i*}=\left(\frac{(\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h})^{\top}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h}}{N}\right)^{1/2}Z,

where
Z\sim N_{r_{h}}(0,\bm{I}_{r_{h}})
. Furthermore, the set of random vectors
\{[\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h}]_{i*}:i\in[N]\}
are independent.
From the definition (
F.94
), we can conclude that for all
N\in\mathbb{N}

\frac{(\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h})^{\top}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h}}{N}=\bm{T}^{G,L}_{t,0}.

Therefore, using Proposition
5.1
and the Strong Law of Large Numbers we can conclude (
2
). The Assertions (3)-(6) for
\{{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h}:h\in[m]\}
follows by retracing the techniques used to prove Lemma E.1 of
[
34
]
. We omit the details to avoid the repetition of similar arguments. Furthermore, Assertion (1) for
\{{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{1,h}:h\in[m]\}
follows using the techniques used to prove Lemma E.1 of
[
34
]
. Proceeding as in the proof of (3.36) of
[
34
]
, we can show that

{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{1,h}|_{\mathcal{G}_{1,0}}=\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h}+o(1)\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h}.

Since
\frac{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}{p_{h}}\rightarrow\lambda_{R,h}\in(0,1]
, using (
5.6
) and Cauchy Schwarz inequality we can get constants
C_{h}>0
(possibly depending on
\lambda_{R,h}
) such that

\displaystyle\lim_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{R,h}}\phi_{h}\left((\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h}+o(1)\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h})_{i*},({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}\right)

(F.186)

\displaystyle\hskip 70.0001pt-\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{R,h}}\phi_{h}\left((\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h})_{i*},({\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*},(\bm{V}_{h})_{i*}\right)\Bigg|

(F.187)

\displaystyle\leq C_{h}\left(1+\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}+\frac{\|\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}+\frac{\|{\bm{V}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}+\frac{\|\bm{V}_{h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}\right)\times\frac{\|\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{0,h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}\times o(1)\xrightarrow{a.s}0.

(F.188)

Consequently, in (
F.186
), given
\mathcal{G}_{1,0}
, we can replace
{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{1,h}
by
\widetilde{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{1,h}
for all
h\in[m]
defined as follows

\widetilde{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{1,h}|_{\mathcal{G}_{1,0}}\overset{d}{=}\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{0,h}.

Consequently, using Theorem 3 of
[
5
]
, Proposition
5.1
and retracing the steps of the proof of Lemma E.1 of
[
34
]
, we can show (
F.186
). Again, one can show Assertions (3)-(6) for
{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{1,h}
by retracing the steps of the proof of Lemma E.1 of
[
34
]
. Now, let us assume that Assertions (1)-(6) hold for
t=0,\ldots,s-1
. We shall show that they also hold for
t=s
. In fact, we shall only show Assertion (2) for
t=s
, as the proofs of all other assertions are similar to the proof of Lemma E.1 of
[
34
]
. To show the Assertion 2 for
{\bm{N}}^{G,L}_{s,h}
, observe that, since
\frac{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}{N}\rightarrow\lambda_{L}
, using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and (
5.1
), we have a constant
C>0
(possibly depending on
\lambda_{L}
), such that

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\Bigg(\{(\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}_{L,s,h}\bm{\Xi}^{R}_{s,h}+\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{s,h}}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{s,h}+\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}}_{R,s,h}\,o(1))_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},

(F.189)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt-\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\Bigg(\{(\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}_{L,s,h}\bm{\Xi}^{R}_{s,h}+\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{s,h}}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{s,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},

(F.190)

\displaystyle\hskip 130.0002pt\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\Bigg)\Bigg|,

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\left\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}_{L,s,h}\bm{\Xi}^{R}_{t,h}+\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{s,h}}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{s,h}\right\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}+\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}}_{R,s,h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}+\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\|\bm{U}_{h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}\right.

(F.191)

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 60.00009pt+\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\|{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}\right)\times\left(\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}}_{R,s,h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}\right)\times~o(1).

(F.192)

Furthermore, from induction hypotheses

\displaystyle\limsup_{N\rightarrow\infty}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\left\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}_{L,s,h}\bm{\Xi}^{R}_{t,h}+\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{s,h}}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{s,h}\right\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}<\infty,\quad\limsup_{N\rightarrow\infty}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\|{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}<\infty

(F.193)

\displaystyle\limsup_{N\rightarrow\infty}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\frac{\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}}_{R,s,h}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}<\infty,\quad\limsup_{N\rightarrow\infty}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}<\infty

(F.194)

\displaystyle\mbox{and}\quad\limsup_{N\rightarrow\infty}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell}\|_{F}}{\sqrt{N}}<\infty.

(F.195)

Therefore, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\Bigg|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\Bigg(\{(\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}_{L,s,h}\bm{\Xi}^{R}_{s,h}+\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{s,h}}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{s,h}+\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}}_{R,s,h}\,o(1))_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},

(F.196)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt-\frac{1}{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{F}_{L}}\psi\Bigg(\{(\boldsymbol{\mathrm{N}}_{L,s,h}\bm{\Xi}^{R}_{s,h}+\widetilde{\bm{G}}_{h}\bm{P}^{\perp}_{\boldsymbol{\widebar{\mathsf{N}}}^{G,R}_{s,h}}\widebar{\bm{N}}^{G,R}_{s,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{({\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},

(F.197)

\displaystyle\hskip 150.00023pt\{(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}},\{(\bm{U}_{h})_{i*}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{(\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{\ell})_{i*}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\Bigg)\Bigg|=0.

(F.198)

Using the above observation, we can conclude (
2
) by using Theorem 3 of
[
5
]
and retracing the steps used to prove Lemma 5 of
[
7
]
and Lemma E.1 of
[
34
]
. Furthermore, (
F.186
) can also be established using similar techniques. We omit the details for the sake of brevity.

Appendix G
Proofs of AMP asymptotics with estimated priors

Using Lemma B.4 in
Zhong et al., [59]
and Lemma
5.2
, we have the following version of Corollary B.5 of
Zhong et al., [59]
.

Lemma G.1
.

For
x=(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m})\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
and
\widetilde{x}=(\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}})\in\mathbb{R}^{\widetilde{r}}

\displaystyle\sup_{(x,\widetilde{x})}\left|f^{L}_{N}\left(x,\widetilde{x};\widehat{\mu}\right)-f^{L}\left(x,\widetilde{x};\mu\right)\right|\rightarrow 0.

(G.1)

Similarly, for all
h\in[m]
,

\displaystyle\sup_{y_{h}\in\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}}}\left|f^{R}_{h,N}(y_{h};\widehat{\nu}_{h})-f^{R}(y_{h};\nu_{h})\right|\rightarrow 0,

(G.2)

where
f^{L}_{N},f^{L},f^{R}_{h,N}
and
f^{R}_{h}
are defined in Lemma
E.1
.

G.1
Proof of Theorem
5.1

We shall prove Theorem
5.1
using the following lemmas characterizing the smoothness of the estimated denoisers. Let us consider the denoisers
u^{\circ}_{t,h},v^{\circ}_{t,h}
defined using the true priors
\mu
and
\nu_{h}
; the state evolution matrices
\{\bm{S}^{L,\circ}_{t,h},\bm{S}^{R,\circ}_{t,h},\bm{\Sigma}^{L,\circ}_{t,h},\bm{\Sigma}^{R,\circ}_{t,h}:t\geq 0,\,h\in[m]\}
and
\{\bm{L}_{\ell}:\ell\in[\widetilde{m}]\}
. Also consider the approximate denoisers constructed using the Empirical Bayes estimates of the priors
\widehat{\mu}
and
\widehat{\nu}_{h}
, namely, defined by (
2.13
), (
2.15
), the state evolution matrices
\{\widehat{\bm{S}}^{L}_{t,h},\widehat{\bm{S}}^{R}_{t,h},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{L}_{t,h},\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{R}_{t,h}:t\geq 0\}
defined by (
2.18
) and the matrices
\{\widehat{\bm{L}}_{\ell}:\ell\in[\widetilde{m}]\}
defined in (
2.10
).

Lemma G.2
.

For all matrices
\bm{F}_{h}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times r_{h}}
,
\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times{\widetilde{r}}_{\ell}}
and
\bm{G}_{h}\in\mathbb{R}^{p_{h}\times r_{h}}
where
h\in[m]
and
\ell\in[\widetilde{m}]
, as
N\rightarrow\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\|u_{t,h}(\bm{F}_{1},\ldots,\bm{F}_{m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}};\widehat{\mu})-u^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{F}_{1},\ldots,\bm{F}_{m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)\|^{2}_{F}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0,

(G.3)

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{h}}\|v_{t,h}(\bm{G}_{h};\widehat{\nu}_{h})-v^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{G}_{h};\nu_{h})\|^{2}_{F}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0.

(G.4)

In the above equations, for all
h\in[m]
the functions
u_{t,h}:\mathbb{R}^{r+\widetilde{r}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}}
and
v_{t,h}:\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}}
are defined in (
2.17
) and the functions
u^{\circ}_{t,h}:\mathbb{R}^{r+\widetilde{r}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}}
and
v^{\circ}_{t,h}:\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}}
are defined in (
5.12
).
Similarly, for the functions
\widetilde{u}_{t,h}:\mathbb{R}^{r+\widetilde{r}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{\widetilde{r}_{\ell}}
defined in (
2.21
) and
\widetilde{u}^{\circ}_{t,h}:\mathbb{R}^{r+\widetilde{r}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{\widetilde{r}_{\ell}}
defined in (
5.12
), as
N\rightarrow\infty

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\|\widetilde{u}_{t,h}(\bm{F}_{1},\ldots,\bm{F}_{m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}};\widehat{\mu})-\widetilde{u}^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{F}_{1},\ldots,\bm{F}_{m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)\|^{2}_{F}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0.

(G.5)

Next, we consider the following lemma characterizing the smoothness of the derivatives of the estimated denoisers.

Lemma G.3
.

Consider the same set-up as Lemma
G.2
. Then as
N\rightarrow\infty

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\bm{J}^{L}_{t,h}((\bm{F}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{F}_{m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\widehat{\mu})

(G.6)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{F}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{F}_{m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\|_{\mathrm{op}}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0,

(G.7)

and

\frac{1}{p_{h}}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{h}}\|\bm{J}^{R}_{t,h}((\bm{G}_{h})_{i*};\widehat{\nu}_{h})-\bm{J}^{R,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{G}_{h})_{i*};\nu_{h})\|_{\mathrm{op}}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0,

for all matrices
\bm{F}_{h}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times r_{h}}
,
\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times{\widetilde{r}}_{\ell}}
and
\bm{G}_{h}\in\mathbb{R}^{p_{h}\times r_{h}}
where
h\in[m]
and
\ell\in[\widetilde{m}]
.

Proof of Theorem
5.1
.

We shall prove the first two parts of the theorem using induction. Let us formulate two sets of induction hypotheses
\mathcal{H}_{t}
and
\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{t}
for
t\in\mathbb{N}\cup\{0\}
, where
\mathcal{H}_{t}
implies:

1.

\widehat{\bm{S}}^{R}_{t,h}\rightarrow\bm{S}^{R,\circ}_{t,h}
and
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{R}_{t,h}\rightarrow\bm{\Sigma}^{R,\circ}_{t,h}
as
N\rightarrow\infty
,

2.

As
N\rightarrow\infty
,
\frac{1}{N}\|\widebar{\bm{U}}_{t-1,h}-\widebar{\bm{U}}^{\circ}_{t-1,h}\|^{2}_{F}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0
,

3.

As
N\rightarrow\infty
,
\frac{1}{N}\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{t-1,\ell}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\circ}_{t-1,\ell}\|^{2}_{F}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0
,
for
t\geq 1
,

4.

As
N\rightarrow\infty
,
\frac{1}{p_{h}}\|\bm{V}_{t,h}-\bm{V}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0
,
for all
h\in[m],\;\ell\in[\widetilde{m}]
,

and
\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{t}
implies:

1.

\widehat{\bm{S}}^{L}_{t,h}\rightarrow\bm{S}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}
and
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{L}_{t,h}\rightarrow\bm{\Sigma}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}
as
N\rightarrow\infty
.

2.

As
p_{h}\rightarrow\infty
,
\frac{1}{p_{h}}\|\widebar{\bm{V}}_{t,h}-\widebar{\bm{V}}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0
,

3.

As
p_{h}\rightarrow\infty
,
\frac{1}{N}\|\bm{U}_{t,h}-\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0
,
for all
h\in[m]
.

Step 1: Initialization
\mathcal{H}_{0}
.

By (
2.7
), (
2.8
) and the results from
[
6
]
, the first assertion holds. For the second assertion observe the following:

\frac{1}{N}\|\widebar{\bm{U}}_{-1,h}-\widebar{\bm{U}}^{\circ}_{-1,h}\|^{2}_{F}\leq\frac{1}{N}\|{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\|^{2}_{F}\;\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{R,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{1/2}-(\bm{\Sigma}^{R,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{1/2}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}.

By Proposition
5.1
,
\limsup\limits_{N\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{N}\|{\bm{U}}^{\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\|^{2}_{F}<\infty
almost surely. Finally, since the plug-in estimator of
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{R,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}
converges to
\bm{\Sigma}^{R,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}
as
N\rightarrow\infty
, the second assertion holds. Further, as both algorithms, given by (
2.16
) and (
5.13
), have the same initialization for
\bm{V}_{0,h}
for all
h\in[m]
and
\ell\in[\widetilde{m}]
, the fourth assertions also hold. The third assertion is not applicable for
\mathcal{H}_{0}
.

Step 2:
\mathcal{H}_{t}\implies\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{t}
.

This inductive step follows using the arguments of the proof of Theorem 5.4 in
Zhong et al., [59]
.

Step 3:
\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{t}\implies\mathcal{H}_{t+1}
.

First, let us consider the second assertion. Observe that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\|\widebar{\bm{U}}_{t,h}-\widebar{\bm{U}}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}

(G.8)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\|u_{t,h}(\bm{U}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}};\widehat{\mu})-u^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)\|^{2}_{F}

(G.9)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|u_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\widehat{\mu})

(G.10)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-u^{\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\|^{2}_{2}

(G.11)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|u_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\widehat{\mu})

(G.12)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-u^{\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\|^{2}_{2}

(G.13)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|u^{\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)

(G.14)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-u^{\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\|^{2}_{2}.

(G.15)

Let us consider the first expression on the right-hand side of the above equation. By Lemma
G.2
we have:

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|u_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\widehat{\mu})

(G.16)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-u^{\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\|^{2}_{2}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0.

(G.17)

Next, for second expression on the right-hand side of (
G.8
), we use the Lipschitz property of the denoisers implied by Assumption
5.1
, to conclude that for all
h\in[m]

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|u^{\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)

(G.18)

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-u^{\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\|^{2}_{2}

(G.19)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{N}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\|\bm{U}_{t,h}-\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0,\quad\mbox{as $N\rightarrow\infty$}.

(G.20)

Here
C_{1}>0
is an absolute constant and the last relation follows using
\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{t}(3)
. For the third assertion, observe that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\|\widetilde{\bm{U}}_{t,h}-\widetilde{\bm{U}}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\|\widetilde{u}_{t,h}(\bm{U}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}};\widehat{\mu})-\widetilde{u}^{\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\widetilde{u}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\widehat{\mu})

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-\widetilde{u}^{\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\|^{2}_{2}.

(G.21)

The right-hand side of (
G.21
) converges to
0
by (
G.5
), Lemma
G.2
and arguments as before.
Finally, consider the fourth assertion. Define

\bm{J}^{L}_{t,h}\equiv\bm{J}^{L}_{t,h}(\bm{U}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}};\widehat{\mu})

and

\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}\equiv\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,1},\ldots,\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,m},\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu).

We can get a constant
C_{2}>0
such that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{h}}\|\bm{V}_{t+1,h}-\bm{V}^{\circ}_{t+1,h}\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}}{p_{h}}\|\bm{X}^{\top}_{h}\widebar{\bm{U}}_{t,h}-\bm{X}^{\top}_{h}\widebar{\bm{U}}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}+C_{2}\frac{\|\widebar{\bm{V}}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}}{p_{h}}\|\bm{J}^{L}_{t,h}-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}

(G.22)

\displaystyle\hskip 30.00005pt+\frac{C_{2}}{p_{h}}\|\widebar{\bm{V}}_{t,h}-\widebar{\bm{V}}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}\|\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}

(G.23)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}}{p_{h}}\|\bm{X}_{h}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}\|\widebar{\bm{U}}_{t,h}-\widebar{\bm{U}}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}+C_{2}\frac{\|\widebar{\bm{V}}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}}{p_{h}}\|\bm{J}^{L}_{t,h}-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}

(G.24)

\displaystyle\hskip 30.00005pt+\frac{C_{2}}{p_{h}}\|\widebar{\bm{V}}_{t,h}-\widebar{\bm{V}}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}\|\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}.

(G.25)

By Corollary 5.35 of
Vershynin, [56]
we have
\limsup_{N\rightarrow\infty}\|\bm{X}_{h}\|_{\mathrm{op}}<\infty
almost surely. By
\mathcal{\bar{H}}_{t}(2)
,
\mathcal{H}_{t}(2)
and Theorem
5.1
, we can conclude that
\limsup_{p_{h}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{\|\widebar{\bm{V}}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}}{p_{h}}<\infty
, almost surely. Further, observe that

\displaystyle\bm{J}^{L}_{t,h}-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}

(G.26)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bigg[\bm{J}^{L}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\widehat{\mu})

(G.27)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\bigg]

(G.28)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bigg[\bm{J}^{L}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\widehat{\mu})

(G.29)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\bigg]

(G.30)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bigg[\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)

(G.31)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\bigg].

(G.32)

For the expression in (
G.29
), using Lemma
G.3
can conclude that

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bigg[\bm{J}^{L}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\widehat{\mu})

(G.33)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\bigg]\Bigg\|_{\mathrm{op}}

(G.34)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bigg\|\bm{J}^{L}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\widehat{\mu})

(G.35)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\bigg\|_{\mathrm{op}}

(G.36)

\displaystyle\qquad\xrightarrow{a.s.}0,\quad\mbox{as $N\rightarrow\infty$.}

(G.37)

To analyze the expression in (
G.31
), we first express the function
u^{\circ}_{t,h}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)
using Tweedie’s formula as follows:

\displaystyle u^{\circ}_{t,h}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)=x_{h}+\frac{\nabla_{h}f^{L}_{t}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)}{f^{L}_{t}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)},

(G.38)

where
\nabla_{h}
refers to the gradient of a function
f:\mathbb{R}^{r+\widetilde{r}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
with respect to the vector of argument
x_{h}
. The function
f^{L}_{t}:\mathbb{R}^{r+\widetilde{r}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
is defined as follows:

\displaystyle f^{L}_{t}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)\equiv

\displaystyle\int_{u,\tilde{u}}\prod_{h=1}^{m}\phi_{r_{h}}((\bm{\Sigma}^{L,\circ}_{t,h})^{-1/2}(x_{h}-\bm{S}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}u_{h}))\times

(G.39)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt\prod_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\phi_{\widetilde{r}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{x}_{\ell}-\bm{L}_{\ell}\widetilde{u}_{\ell})d\mu(u_{1},\ldots,u_{m},\widetilde{u}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{u}_{\ell}).

(G.40)

By Assumption
5.1
, since
u^{\circ}_{t,h}
is uniformly Lipschitz, therefore
\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}
is uniformly bounded by a constant
C_{L}
. Further, for all
h\in[m]
and
\ell\in[\widetilde{m}]
;
x_{h}\in\mathbb{B}_{r_{h}}(B)
(here,
\mathbb{B}_{r}(B)
denotes the open Euclidean ball of dimension
r
and radius
B
) and
\widetilde{x}_{\ell}\in\mathbb{B}_{{\widetilde{r}}_{\ell}}(B)
, then there
exists
a constant
c>0
, such that
f_{t}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)>c
which implies that the derivative of
\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}
with respect to the variable
x_{h}
is uniformly bounded by a constant
C_{B}>0
for all
x_{h}\in\mathbb{B}_{r_{h}}(B)
and
\widetilde{x}_{\ell}\in\mathbb{B}_{{\widetilde{r}}_{\ell}}(B)
.
Hence, if
x_{h}\in\mathbb{B}_{r_{h}}(B)
and
\widetilde{x}_{\ell}\in\mathbb{B}_{{\widetilde{r}}_{\ell}}(B)
for all
h\in[m]
and
\ell\in[\widetilde{m}]
, then
\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}
is Lipschitz continuous with Lipschitz constant
C_{B}
. Now, consider the term in (
G.31
).

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bigg\|\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};{\mu})

(G.41)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\bigg\|_{\mathrm{op}}

(G.42)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bigg\|\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};{\mu})

(G.43)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\bigg\|_{\mathrm{op}}

(G.44)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt\times\prod_{h=1}^{m}\mathbb{I}(\|(\bm{U}_{t,h})_{i*}\|_{2}\leq B)\times\prod_{\ell=1}^{n}\mathbb{I}(\|(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\|_{2}\leq B)\times\prod_{h=1}^{m}\mathbb{I}(\|(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,h})_{i*}\|_{2}\leq B)

(G.45)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{C_{L}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\mathbb{I}(\|(\bm{U}_{t,h})_{i*}\|_{2}\geq B)+\frac{C_{L}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\mathbb{I}(\|(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\|_{2}\geq B)

(G.46)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt+\frac{C_{L}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\mathbb{I}(\|(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,h})_{i*}\|_{2}\geq B).

(G.47)

Since
\|x\|^{2}_{2}\geq B^{2}\mathbb{I}(\|x\|_{2}\geq B)
, for all
x\in\mathbb{R}^{r_{h}}
, the last two terms in the above display can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{C_{L}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\mathbb{I}(\|(\bm{U}_{t,h})_{i*}\|_{2}\geq B)+\frac{C_{L}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\mathbb{I}(\|(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\|_{2}\geq B)

(G.48)

\displaystyle\hskip 200.0003pt+\frac{C_{L}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\mathbb{I}(\|(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,h})_{i*}\|_{2}\geq B)

(G.49)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{L}}{NB^{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\|\bm{U}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{C_{L}}{NB^{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\|\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{C_{L}}{NB^{2}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\|\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell}\|^{2}_{F}.

(G.50)

By definition of
\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell}
,
\mathcal{\widebar{H}}_{t}(3)
and Theorem
F.1
, we can conclude that, almost surely

\limsup_{N\rightarrow\infty}\left\{\frac{C_{L}}{N}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\|\bm{U}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{C_{L}}{N}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\|\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{C_{L}}{N}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\|\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell}\|^{2}_{F}\right\}<\infty.

Hence, if we take
B\rightarrow\infty
, we get

\displaystyle\limsup_{B\rightarrow\infty}\left\{\frac{C_{L}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\mathbb{I}(\|(\bm{U}_{t,h})_{i*}\|_{2}\geq B)+\frac{C_{L}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\mathbb{I}(\|(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\|_{2}\geq B)\right.

(G.51)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt\left.+\frac{C_{L}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\mathbb{I}(\|(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,h})_{i*}\|_{2}\geq B)\right\}\overset{a.s.}{=}0

(G.52)

To analyze the other term we use the Lipschitz property of the Jacobian matrices of the denoisers when its arguments are confined to an Euclidean ball of appropriate dimensions and radius
B>0
. That condition implies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\bigg\|\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};{\mu})

(G.53)

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}((\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,1})_{i*},\ldots,(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,m})_{i*},(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{1})_{i*},\ldots,(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\widetilde{m}})_{i*};\mu)\bigg\|_{\mathrm{op}}

(G.54)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt\times\prod_{h=1}^{m}\mathbb{I}(\|(\bm{U}_{t,h})_{i*}\|_{2}\leq B)\times\prod_{\ell=1}^{n}\mathbb{I}(\|(\widetilde{\bm{X}}_{\ell})_{i*}\|_{2}\leq B)\times\prod_{h=1}^{m}\mathbb{I}(\|(\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,h})_{i*}\|_{2}\leq B)

(G.55)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{B}}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{h=1}^{m}\|\bm{U}_{t,h}-\bm{U}^{\circ}_{t,h}\|_{F}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0.

(G.56)

Hence, we can conclude that
\|\bm{J}^{L}_{t,h}-\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}\|\xrightarrow{a.s.}0
, as
N\rightarrow\infty
. Using similar arguments one can also show that
\limsup_{N\rightarrow\infty}\|\bm{J}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}\|<\infty
, almost surely. Combining (
G.22
),
\mathcal{H}_{t+1}(2)
and
\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{t}
, the third assertion of
\mathcal{H}_{t+1}
follows.

To show the first assertion of
\mathcal{H}_{t+1}
, we observe that
u^{\otimes 2}_{t,h}
is a pseudo-Lipschitz function. Hence, the assertion follows using the techniques described in the proof of Theorem 5.4 in
Zhong et al., [59]
.

Finally, since
\frac{|\mathcal{F}_{L}|}{N}\rightarrow\lambda_{L}\in(0,1]
and
\frac{|\mathcal{F}_{R,h}|}{p_{h}}\rightarrow\lambda_{R,h}\in(0,1]
for all
h\in[m]
, the third part of the theorem follows using Theorem
F.1
and the property of pseudo-Lipschitz functions (in particular, inequalities of form (
F.159
)).
∎

G.2
Proof of Lemma
G.1

We shall prove the statement (
G.1
). The proof of (
G.2
) is similar. Denote by

\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}=\bigg(\{\widehat{\bm{S}}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\widehat{\bm{L}}_{\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\bigg),

and

\bm{\Theta}=\bigg(\{\bm{S}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\bm{\Sigma}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\bm{L}_{\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\bigg).

Consider the functions

\xi^{L}_{N}((x,\widetilde{x});(u,\widetilde{u});\widehat{\bm{\Theta}})=\prod_{h=1}^{m}\phi_{r_{h}}((\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{-1/2}(x_{h}-\widehat{\bm{S}}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}u_{h}))\prod_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\phi_{\widetilde{r}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{x}_{\ell}-\widehat{\bm{L}}_{\ell}\widetilde{u}_{\ell}),

and

\xi((x,\widetilde{x});(u,\widetilde{u});\bm{\Theta})=\prod_{h=1}^{m}\phi_{r_{h}}((\bm{\Sigma}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{-1/2}(x_{h}-\bm{S}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}u_{h}))\prod_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\phi_{\widetilde{r}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{x}_{\ell}-\bm{L}_{\ell}\widetilde{u}_{\ell}).

Since, by Lemma
5.1
,
\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\rightarrow\bm{\Theta}
as
N\rightarrow\infty
, both
\xi^{L}_{N}((x,\widetilde{x});(u,\widetilde{u});\widehat{\bm{\Theta}})
and
\xi((x,\widetilde{x});(u,\widetilde{u});\bm{\Theta})
and their derivatives are uniformly bounded with respect to
(x,\widetilde{x})
,
(u,\widetilde{u})
and
\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}
, when
\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}
is contained in a finite radius neighborhood of
\bm{\Theta}
.

Then, proceeding as in the proof of Lemma B.4 and Corollary B.5 of
Zhong et al., [59]
, the stated result follows.

G.3
Proof of Lemma
G.2

We shall prove (
G.3
) and the proof of (
G.4
) shall follow similarly. Let us consider the collection of matrices
\bm{\Theta}=\bigg(\{\bm{S}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\bm{\Sigma}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\bm{L}_{\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\bigg)
and
\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}=\bigg(\{\widehat{\bm{S}}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\}_{h=1}^{m},\{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\}_{=1}^{m},\{\widehat{\bm{L}}_{\ell}\}_{\ell=1}^{\widetilde{m}}\bigg)
. By Tweedie’s formula, for
h\in[m]
, we can write the posterior mean denoisers as

\displaystyle u_{t,h}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\widehat{\mu})

(G.57)

\displaystyle=(\widehat{\bm{S}}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{-1}\bigg(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\frac{\nabla_{x_{h}}f^{L}_{N}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\widehat{\mu})^{\top}}{f^{L}_{N}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\widehat{\mu})}+x_{h}\bigg),

(G.58)

and

\displaystyle u^{\circ}_{t,h}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)

(G.59)

\displaystyle=(\bm{S}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h})^{-1}\bigg(\bm{\Sigma}^{L,\mathrm{pc}}_{0,h}\frac{\nabla_{x_{h}}f^{L}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)^{\top}}{f^{L}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)}+x_{h}\bigg),

(G.60)

where for any function
f
defined on appropriate domain,
\nabla_{x_{h}}f(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\widehat{\mu})
denotes the gradient of the function
f
with respect to
x_{h}
. In the above equations the functions
f^{L}_{N}
and
f^{L}
are defined in (
E.1
) and (
E.5
), respectively.
By Lemma
G.1
, for
N\rightarrow\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{(x,\widetilde{x})}\Bigg|f^{L}_{N}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\widehat{\mu})-f^{L}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)\Bigg|\rightarrow 0.

(G.61)

Using similar techniques as the proof of Lemma
G.1
, we can also show that for
h\in[m]
:

\displaystyle\sup_{(x,\widetilde{x})}\Bigg\|\nabla_{x_{h}}f^{L}_{N}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\widehat{\mu})-\nabla_{x_{h}}f^{L}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m},\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{\widetilde{m}};\mu)\Bigg\|_{\mathrm{op}}\rightarrow 0.

(G.62)

Since, by Lemma
5.1
,
\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}\rightarrow\bm{\Theta}
as
N\rightarrow\infty
, we have an open bounded neighborhood of
\bm{\Theta}
such that as
N\rightarrow\infty
, the matrices in the collection
\widehat{\bm{\Theta}}
lie in it. Then using Assumption
5.1
, we can verify along the lines of Proposition B.1 of
Zhong et al., [59]
that there exists a neighborhood of
(\bm{\Theta},\mu)
(with respect to the topology of weak convergence in
\mu
) where
u_{t,h}
is uniformly Lipschitz in
(x,\widetilde{x})
. Therefore, following the analysis of Corollary B.3 of
Zhong et al., [59]
, we can conclude (
G.3
). The proofs of (
G.4
) and (
G.5
) follow using similar arguments.

G.4
Proof of Lemma
G.3

The proof of this Lemma follows using the techniques used to prove Lemma
G.2
and Proposition B.6 of
Zhong et al., [59]
.

Appendix H
Proof of Bayes optimality of orchestrated AMP

H.1
Proof of Lemma
5.3

Recall the definitions of
\bm{\Gamma}^{R}_{t,h}
and
\bm{\Gamma}^{L}_{t,h}
from (
5.24
). Using (
5.9
), we can write

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}^{R}_{t,h}=\bm{D}^{1/2}_{h}\bm{\Sigma}^{R,\circ}_{t,h}\bm{D}^{1/2}_{h},\quad\mbox{and}\quad\bm{\Gamma}^{L}_{t,h}=\frac{1}{\gamma_{h}}\bm{D}^{1/2}_{h}\bm{\Sigma}^{L,\circ}_{t,h}\bm{D}^{1/2}_{h}.

(H.1)

Since
